  GET http://127.0.0.1:65083/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/workflows - Workflows
  POST http://127.0.0.1:65083/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/query - run a read-only SQL query (SELECT/WITH only) over captured request traces and console logs. Tables: spans, logs (read attributes via json(attributes)). Example:
    curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:65083/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/query -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"sql":"SELECT service, name, outcome, duration_ms FROM spans WHERE parent_id IS NULL LIMIT 20"}'
  POST http://127.0.0.1:65083/cdn-cgi/local/explorer/api/local/observability/clear - clear all captured traces and logs
If the routes above don't cover what you need, fetch the full OpenAPI schema (large - use only as a last resort):
